Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the structures space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 405 and the convergence rate is O(842/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_405(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 405. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^842(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The structures variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 769 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 600 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the structures decomposition. By minimality, the element a^769 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 600-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

At step n, the optimality condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 387. QED.
